Vec::new();\n49\t    let mut db_ok = true;\n50\t    let mut jsonl_ok = true;\n51\t    let mut result = DoctorResult::default();\n52\t\n53\t    // Check database\n54\t    let (db_issue_count, db_integrity_ok) = check_database(&db_path)?;\n55\t    result.db_issue_count = db_issue_count;\n56\t    if !db_integrity_ok {\n57\t        db_ok = false;\n58\t        issues.push(\"Database integrity check failed\".to_string());\n59\t    }\n60\t\n61\t    // Check JSONL file\n62\t    let (jsonl_line_count, jsonl_valid) = check_jsonl(&jsonl_path)?;\n63\t    result.jsonl_line_count = jsonl_line_count;\n64\t    if !jsonl_valid {\n65\t        jsonl_ok = false;\n66\t        issues.push(\"JSONL file contains invalid lines\".to_string());\n67\t    }\n68\t\n69\t    // Check consistency with content_hash comparison\n70\t    if db_ok && jsonl_ok {\n71\t        let drift = check_consistency_with_hash(&db_path, &jsonl_path)?;\n72\t\n73\t        let total_drift = drift.missing_in_jsonl.len()\n74\t            + drift.missing_in_sqlite.len()\n75\t            + drift.hash_mismatch.len();\n76\t\n77\t        result.missing_in_jsonl = drift.missing_in_jsonl.clone();\n78\t        result.missing_in_sqlite = drift.missing_in_sqlite.clone();\n79\t        result.hash_mismatch = drift.hash_mismatch.clone();\n80\t\n81\t        if total_drift > 0 {\n82\t            issues.push(format!(\n83\t                \"Drift detected: {} missing in JSONL, {} missing in SQLite, {} hash mismatch\",\n84\t                result.missing_in_jsonl.len(),\n85\t                result.missing_in_sqlite.len(),\n86\t                result.hash_mismatch.len()\n87\t            ));\n88\t        }\n89\t\n90\t        // Count unflushed beads\n91\t        let unflushed_count = count_unflushed(&db_path)?;\n92\t        result.unflushed_count = unflushed_count;\n93\t\n94\t        if unflushed_count > 0 {\n95\t            issues.push(format!(\n96\t                \"{} unflushed bead(s) exist (modified or created since last flush to JSONL)\",\n97\t                unflushed_count\n98\t            ));\n99\t        }\n100\t    }\n101\t\n102\t    // Check for beads stuck in a done-sounding custom status instead of closed/tombstone\n103\t    // (bf-wre). Independent of db_ok/jsonl_ok since it only needs the sqlite db to be\n104\t    // openable, not a full consistency check.\n105\t    let pseudo_terminal_ids = check_pseudo_terminal_statuses(&db_path)?;\n106\t    if !pseudo_terminal_ids.is_empty() {\n107\t        issues.push(format!(\n108\t            \"{} bead(s) have a done-sounding status (e.g. \\\"completed\\\") instead of \\\"closed\\\" \\\n109\t             -- run `bf close <id>` on each to fully close them: {}\",\n110\t            pseudo_terminal_ids.len(),\n111\t            pseudo_terminal_ids.join(\", \")\n112\t        ));\n113\t    }\n114\t    result.pseudo_terminal_status_ids = pseudo_terminal_ids;\n115\t\n116\t    result.db_ok = db_ok;\n117\t    result.jsonl_ok = jsonl_ok;\n118\t    result.issues = issues;\n119\t\n120\t    Ok(result)\n121\t}\n122\t\n123\t/// Find beads whose status is a `TERMINAL_STATUS_ALIASES` value (e.g. \"completed\",\n124\t/// \"done\") rather than the canonical \"closed\"/\"tombstone\". These beads are functionally\n125\t/// terminal (see `Status::is_terminal()`) but weren't actually closed, so `bf show`/`bf\n126\t/// list` still displays them as active and `closed_at`/`close_reason` are never set.\n127\tfn check_pseudo_terminal_statuses(db_path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<String>> {\n128\t    let conn = Connection::open(db_path)?;\n129\t    let placeholders = crate::model::TERMINAL_STATUS_ALIASES\n130\t        .iter()\n131\t        .map(|s| format!(\"'{s}'\"))\n132\t        .collect::<Vec<_>>()\n133\t        .join(\", \");\n134\t    let query = format!(\n135\t        \"SELECT id FROM issues WHERE LOWER(status) IN ({placeholders}) AND deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY id\"\n136\t    );\n137\t    let mut stmt = conn.prepare(&query)?;\n138\t    let ids = stmt\n139\t        .query_map([], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0))?\n140\t        .collect::<rusqlite::Result<Vec<_>>>()?;\n141\t    Ok(ids)\n142\t}\n143\t\n144\t/// Check database integrity.\n145\tfn check_database(db_path: &Path) -> Result<(usize, bool)> {\n146\t    let conn = Connection::open(db_path)?;\n147\t\n148\t    // Apply schema if database is new (no tables yet)\n149\t    let needs_schema: bool = conn\n150\t        .query_row(\n151\t            \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='issues'\",\n152\t            [],\n153\t            |row| row.get::<_, i64>(0),\n154\t        )\n155\t        .map(|n| n == 0)\n156\t        .unwrap_or(true);\n157\t    if needs_schema {\n158\t        crate::storage::schema::apply_schema(&conn)?;\n159\t    }\n160\t\n161\t    // Run PRAGMA integrity_check\n162\t    let integrity_result: String =\n163\t        conn.query_row(\"PRAGMA integrity_check\", [], |row| row.get(0))?;\n164\t\n165\t    let integrity_ok = integrity_result == \"ok\";\n166\t\n167\t    // Count issues\n168\t    let count: i64 = conn.query_row(\n169\t        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM issues WHERE deleted_at IS NULL\",\n170\t        [],\n171\t        |row| row.get(0),\n172\t    )?;\n173\t\n174\t    Ok((count as usize, integrity_ok))\n175\t}\n176\t\n177\t/// Check JSONL file validity.\n178\tfn check_jsonl(jsonl_path: &Path) -> Result<(usize, bool)> {\n179\t    if !jsonl_path.exists() {\n180\t        return Ok((0, true)); // Empty workspace is valid\n181\t    }\n182\t\n183\t    let mut count = 0;\n184\t    let mut valid = true;\n185\t\n186\t    match stream_issues(jsonl_path) {\n187\t        Ok(iter) => {\n188\t            for result in iter {\n189\t                match result {\n190\t                    Ok(_) => count += 1,\n191\t                    Err(e) => {\n192\t                        valid = false;\n193\t                        eprintln!(\"Invalid JSONL line: {}\", e);\n194\t                    }\n195\t                }\n196\t            }\n197\t        }\n198\t        Err(e) => {\n199\t            return Err(anyhow!(\"Failed to read JSONL file: {}\", e));\n200\t        }\n201\t    }\n202\t\n203\t    Ok((count, valid))\n204\t}\n205\t\n206\t/// Consistency drift details.\n207\t#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]\n208\tstruct ConsistencyDrift {\n209\t    /// Bead IDs that exist in SQLite but not in JSONL\n210\t    missing_in_jsonl: Vec<String>,\n211\t    /// Bead IDs that exist in JSONL but not in SQLite\n212\t    missing_in_sqlite: Vec<String>,\n213\t    /// Bead IDs where content_hash differs between JSONL and SQLite\n214\t    hash_mismatch: Vec<String>,\n215\t}\n216\t\n217\t/// Check consistency between database and JSONL using content_hash.\n218\t///\n219\t/// Compares each bead's content_hash between JSONL and SQLite to detect drift.\n220\tfn check_consistency_with_hash(db_path: &Path, jsonl_path: &Path) -> Result<ConsistencyDrift> {\n221\t    use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};\n222\t\n223\t    let storage = Storage::open(db_path)?;\n224\t\n225\t    // Build a map of all SQLite issues with their content_hash\n226\t    let sqlite_issues: HashMap<String, String> = storage\n227\t        .list_all_issues()?\n228\t        .into_iter()\n229\t        .map(|issue| {\n230\t            let hash = issue.content_hash();\n231\t            (issue.id, hash)\n232\t        })\n233\t        .collect();\n234\t\n235\t    let mut drift = ConsistencyDrift::default();\n236\t    let mut jsonl_seen: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();\n237\t\n238\t    // Stream JSONL and compare, tracking seen IDs\n239\t    if jsonl_path.exists() {\n240\t        let iter = stream_issues(jsonl_path)?;\n241\t        for result in iter {\n242\t            let jsonl_issue = result?;\n243\t            let jsonl_hash = jsonl_issue.content_hash();\n244\t            let bead_id = jsonl_issue.id.clone();\n245\t            jsonl_seen.insert(bead_id.clone());\n246\t\n247\t            match sqlite_issues.get(&bead_id) {\n248\t                Some(sqlite_hash) => {\n249\t                    if sqlite_hash != &jsonl_hash {\n250\t                        drift.hash_mismatch.push(bead_id);\n251\t                    }\n252\t                }\n253\t                None => {\n254\t                    drift.missing_in_sqlite.push(bead_id);\n255\t                }\n256\t            }\n257\t        }\n258\t    }\n259\t\n260\t    // Find beads in SQLite but not in JSONL (using tracked seen IDs)\n261\t    for bead_id in sqlite_issues.keys() {\n262\t        if !jsonl_seen.contains(bead_id) {\n263\t            drift.missing_in_jsonl.push(bead_id.clone());\n264\t        }\n265\t    }\n266\t\n267\t    Ok(drift)\n268\t}\n269\t\n270\t/// Count unflushed beads (db-only or db-newer beads not yet flushed to JSONL).\n271\t///\n272\t/// Returns the count of beads that exist in SQLite but have been marked as dirty\n273\t/// (modified or created since the last flush to JSONL).\n274\tpub fn count_unflushed(db_path: &Path) -> Result<usize> {\n275\t    use rusqlite::Connection;\n276\t\n277\t    let conn = Connection::open(db_path)?;\n278\t\n279\t    // Check if dirty_issues table exists\n280\t    let table_exists: i64 = conn.query_row(\n281\t        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='dirty_issues'\",\n282\t        [],\n283\t        |row| row.get(0),\n284\t    )?;\n285\t\n286\t    if table_exists == 0 {\n287\t        return Ok(0);\n288\t    }\n289\t\n290\t    let count: i64 = conn.query_row(\n291\t        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dirty_issues\",\n292\t        [],\n293\t        |row| row.get(0),\n294\t    )?;\n295\t\n296\t    Ok(count as usize)\n297\t}\n298\t\n299\t/// Get IDs of unflushed beads.\n300\t///\n301\t/// Returns a list of bead IDs that exist in SQLite but have been marked as dirty\n302\t/// (modified or created since the last flush to JSONL).\n303\tfn get_unflushed_ids(db_path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<String>> {\n304\t    use rusqlite::Connection;\n305\t\n306\t    let conn = Connection::open(db_path)?;\n307\t\n308\t    // Check if dirty_issues table exists\n309\t    let table_exists: i64 = conn.query_row(\n310\t        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='dirty_issues'\",\n311\t        [],\n312\t        |row| row.get(0),\n313\t    )?;\n314\t\n315\t    if table_exists == 0 {\n316\t        return Ok(Vec::new());\n317\t    }\n318\t\n319\t    let mut stmt = conn.prepare(\"SELECT issue_id FROM dirty_issues ORDER BY issue_id\")?;\n320\t    let mut rows = stmt.query([])?;\n321\t\n322\t    let mut ids = Vec::new();\n323\t    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n324\t        ids.push(row.get(0)?);\n325\t    }\n326\t\n327\t    Ok(ids)\n328\t}\n329\t\n330\t/// Repair the database by rebuilding from JSONL.\n331\t///\n332\t/// This is the recovery operation when the database is corrupted or missing.\n333\t/// The JSONL file is the authoritative source of truth for repair.\n334\t///\n335\t/// # Arguments\n336\t/// * `workspace_dir` - Path to the workspace root (contains .beads/)\n337\t/// * `flush_first` - If true, flush unflushed beads to JSONL before repairing\n338\t/// * `force` - If true, proceed even with unflushed beads (they will be lost)\n339\t///\n340\t/// # Returns\n341\t/// * `Ok(usize)` - Number of beads imported\n342\tpub fn repair(workspace_dir: &Path, flush_first: bool, force: bool) -> Result<usize> {\n343\t    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir)\n344\t        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display()))?;\n345\t    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n346\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n347\t    let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.jsonl_export);\n348\t\n349\t    // Check if JSONL exists\n350\t    if !jsonl_path.exists() {\n351\t        return Err(anyhow!(\n352\t            \"Cannot repair: JSONL file not found at {}\",\n353\t            jsonl_path.display()\n354\t        ));\n355\t    }\n356\t\n357\t    // Check for unflushed beads if database exists and is valid\n358\t    // If db is corrupted, we can't detect unflushed beads - proceed with warning\n359\t    let (unflushed_ids, db_corrupted) = if db_path.exists() {\n360\t        match get_unflushed_ids(&db_path) {\n361\t            Ok(ids) => (ids, false),\n362\t            Err(_) => {\n363\t                // Database is corrupted or unreadable\n364\t                // We can't detect unflushed beads, so proceed with a warning\n365\t                if flush_first {\n366\t                    // Cannot flush from a corrupt database\n367\t                    return Err(anyhow!(\n368\t                        \"Cannot flush: database is corrupted and unreadable.\\n\\\n369\t                         Flushing from a corrupt DB would poison the JSONL checkpoint.\\n\\\n370\t                         Unflushed beads cannot be recovered.\\n\\\n371\t                         Remove --flush-first to proceed with repair only.\"\n372\t                    ));\n373\t                }\n374\t                // Proceed without unflushed check (db is unreadable)\n375\t                // No --force needed since we can't detect unflushed beads anyway\n376\t                eprintln!(\"WARNING: Database is corrupted and unreadable.\");\n377\t                eprintln!(\"  Cannot detect unflushed beads - any db-only beads will be lost.\");\n378\t                eprintln!(\"  Proceeding with repair from JSONL...\");\n379\t                (Vec::new(), true)\n380\t            }\n381\t        }\n382\t    } else {\n383\t        // DB doesn't exist - no unflushed beads possible\n384\t        (Vec::new(), false)\n385\t    };\n386\t\n387\t    if !unflushed_ids.is_empty() {\n388\t        if flush_first {\n389\t            // Flush ALL beads to JSONL first (not just dirty ones)\n390\t            // We must export everything because repair will rebuild db from JSONL\n391\t            eprintln!(\"Flushing all beads to JSONL before repair (including {} unflushed)...\", unflushed_ids.len());\n392\t            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n393\t            let flushed = storage.sync_to_jsonl(&jsonl_path, false)?;\n394\t            eprintln!(\"Flushed {} bead(s) to JSONL\", flushed);\n395\t        } else if !force {\n396\t            // Refuse to proceed with unflushed beads\n397\t            return Err(anyhow!(\n398\t                \"Cannot repair: {} unflushed bead(s) exist ({}).\\n\\\n399\t                 Run 'bf doctor --repair --flush-first' to flush before repair,\\n\\\n400\t                 or 'bf doctor --repair --force' to proceed (these beads will be LOST).\\n\\\n401\t                 Unflushed beads: {}\",\n402\t                unflushed_ids.len(),\n403\t                if unflushed_ids.len() <= 5 {\n404\t                    unflushed_ids.iter().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(\", \")\n405\t                } else {\n406\t                    format!(\n407\t                        \"{}, ... and {} more\",\n408\t                        unflushed_ids[..5].join(\", \"),\n409\t                        unflushed_ids.len() - 5\n410\t                    )\n411\t                },\n412\t                unflushed_ids.join(\", \")\n413\t            ));\n414\t        } else {\n415\t            // Force mode: warn but proceed\n416\t            eprintln!(\n417\t                \"WARNING: {} unflushed bead(s) will be LOST: {}\",\n418\t                unflushed_ids.len(),\n419\t                if unflushed_ids.len() <= 5 {\n420\t                    unflushed_ids.iter().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(\", \")\n421\t                } else {\n422\t                    format!(\n423\t                        \"{}, ... and {} more\",\n424\t                        unflushed_ids[..5].join(\", \"),\n425\t                        unflushed_ids.len() - 5\n426\t                    )\n427\t                }\n428\t            );\n429\t            eprintln!(\"Proceeding with repair due to --force flag\");\n430\t        }\n431\t    }\n432\t\n433\t    // Backup existing database if it exists\n434\t    if db_path.exists() {\n435\t        let backup_path = db_path.with_extension(&format!(\n436\t            \"db.backup.{}\",\n437\t            chrono::Utc::now().format(\"%Y%m%d%H%M%S\")\n438\t        ));\n439\t        std::fs::copy(&db_path, &backup_path)?;\n440\t        eprintln!(\"Backed up existing database to {}\", backup_path.display());\n441\t    }\n442\t\n443\t    // Delete old database\n444\t    if db_path.exists() {\n445\t        std::fs::remove_file(&db_path)?;\n446\t    }\n447\t\n448\t    // Create new database and import JSONL\n449\t    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n450\t\n451\t    let result = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n452\t        import_jsonl(&jsonl_path, |issue| {\n453\t            Storage::create_issue_tx(tx, &issue)?;\n454\t            Ok(UpsertResult::New)\n455\t        })\n456\t    })?;\n457\t\n458\t    // Rebuild blocked cache\n459\t    storage.rebuild_blocked_cache()?;\n460\t\n461\t    // Clear dirty marks - after rebuild from JSONL, db and JSONL are in sync\n462\t    // (create_issue_tx doesn't mark dirty, but we clear for safety)\n463\t    storage.clear_dirty()?;\n464\t\n465\t    Ok(result.imported)\n466\t}\n467\t\n468\t/// Rebuild the blocked issues cache.\n469\t///\n470\t/// This materialized view should be rebuilt after dependency or status changes.\n471\tpub fn rebuild_cache(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {\n472\t    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir)\n473\t        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display()))?;\n474\t    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n475\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n476\t\n477\t    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n478\t    storage.rebuild_blocked_cache()?;\n479\t\n480\t    Ok(())\n481\t}\n482\t\n483\t/// Reclaim stale in_progress beads.\n484\t///\n485\t/// Resets beads that have been in_progress for longer than the TTL\n486\t/// back to open status.\n487\t///\n488\t/// # Arguments\n489\t/// * `workspace_dir` - Path to the workspace root (contains .beads/)\n490\t/// * `ttl_minutes` - TTL in minutes (default from config is 30)\n491\t///\n492\t/// # Returns\n493\t/// * `Ok(usize)` - Number of beads reclaimed\n494\tpub fn reclaim_stale(workspace_dir: &Path, ttl_minutes: i64) -> Result<usize> {\n495\t    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir)\n496\t        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display()))?;\n497\t    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n498\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n499\t\n500\t    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n501\t\n502\t    let reclaimed = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n503\t        let stale_cutoff = chrono::Utc::now() - chrono::Duration::minutes(ttl_minutes);\n504\t\n505\t        let reclaimed = tx.execute(\n506\t            \"UPDATE issues\n507\t             SET status = 'open', assignee = NULL, updated_at = ?\n508\t             WHERE status = 'in_progress' AND updated_at < ?\",\n509\t            rusqlite::params![chrono::Utc::now().to_rfc3339(), stale_cutoff.to_rfc3339()],\n510\t        )?;\n511\t\n512\t        Ok::<_, anyhow::Error>(reclaimed)\n513\t    })?;\n514\t\n515\t    Ok(reclaimed)\n516\t}\n517\t\n518\t/// Initialize a new database from an existing JSONL file.\n519\t///\n520\t/// Use this to create a fresh database from a JSONL export without\n521\t/// affecting the existing database.\n522\t///\n523\t/// # Arguments\n524\t/// * `workspace_dir` - Path to the workspace root (contains .beads/)\n525\t/// * `jsonl_path` - Path to the JSONL file to import from\n526\t///\n527\t/// # Returns\n528\t/// * `Ok(usize)` - Number of beads imported\n529\tpub fn init_from_jsonl(workspace_dir: &Path, jsonl_path: &Path) -> Result<usize> {\n530\t    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir)\n531\t        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display()))?;\n532\t    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n533\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n534\t\n535\t    // Check if JSONL exists\n536\t    if !jsonl_path.exists() {\n537\t        return Err(anyhow!(\"JSONL file not found at {}\", jsonl_path.display()));\n538\t    }\n539\t\n540\t    // Create new database and import JSONL\n541\t    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n542\t\n543\t    let result = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n544\t        import_jsonl(jsonl_path, |issue| {\n545\t            Storage::create_issue_tx(tx, &issue)?;\n546\t            Ok(UpsertResult::New)\n547\t        })\n548\t    })?;\n549\t\n550\t    // Rebuild blocked cache\n551\t    storage.rebuild_blocked_cache()?;\n552\t\n553\t    // Clear dirty marks - after import from JSONL, db and JSONL are in sync\n554\t    // (create_issue_tx doesn't mark dirty, but we clear for safety)\n555\t    storage.clear_dirty()?;\n556\t\n557\t    Ok(result.imported)\n558\t}\n559\t\n560\t/// Verify database schema version.\n561\t///\n562\t/// Checks that all required tables and indexes exist.\n563\tpub fn verify_schema(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<bool> {\n564\t    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir)\n565\t        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display()))?;\n566\t    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n567\t    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n568\t\n569\t    let conn = Connection::open(&db_path)?;\n570\t\n571\t    // Check for critical tables\n572\t    let tables = [\n573\t        \"issues\",\n574\t        \"dependencies\",\n575\t        \"labels\",\n576\t        \"comments\",\n577\t        \"events\",\n578\t        \"config\",\n579\t        \"metadata\",\n580\t        \"dirty_issues\",\n581\t        \"export_hashes\",\n582\t        \"blocked_issues_cache\",\n583\t        \"child_counters\",\n584\t    ];\n585\t\n586\t    for table in &tables {\n587\t        let exists: i64 = conn.query_row(\n588\t            \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name=?1\",\n589\t            &[table],\n590\t            |row| row.get(0),\n591\t        )?;\n592\t\n593\t        if exists == 0 {\n594\t Doctor and repair operations for bead-forge.\n//!\n//! Provides health checking and recovery operations for bead databases,\n//! including corruption detection and JSONL-based repair.\n\nuse crate::config::{find_beads_dir, load_metadata};\nuse crate::jsonl::{import_jsonl, stream_issues, UpsertResult};\nuse crate::storage::Storage;\nuse anyhow::{anyhow, Result};\nuse rusqlite::Connection;\nuse std::path::Path;\n\n/// Doctor check results.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]\npub struct DoctorResult {\n    pub db_ok: bool,\n    pub jsonl_ok: bool,\n    pub jsonl_line_count: usize,\n    pub db_issue_count: usize,\n    pub issues: Vec<String>,\n    // Drift tracking\n    pub missing_in_jsonl: Vec<String>,\n    pub missing_in_sqlite: Vec<String>,\n    pub hash_mismatch: Vec<String>,\n    // Unflushed bead tracking (db-only or db-newer beads that haven't been flushed to JSONL)\n    pub unflushed_count: usize,\n    // Beads with a custom status (e.g. \"completed\") that reads as done but isn't the\n    // canonical \"closed\"/\"tombstone\" -- see bf-wre. Status::is_terminal() now treats\n    // these as terminal for blocking/scheduling purposes, but they still display as\n    // non-terminal everywhere else, so this check surfaces them for manual `bf close`.\n    pub pseudo_terminal_status_ids: Vec<String>,\n}\n\n/// Perform a health check on the bead database and JSONL file.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `workspace_dir` - Path to the workspace root (contains .beads/)\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(DoctorResult)` - Health check results\npub fn check(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<DoctorResult> {\n    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir)\n        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display()))?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.jsonl_export);\n\n    let mut issues = Vec::new();\n    let mut db_ok = true;\n    let mut jsonl_ok = true;\n    let mut result = DoctorResult::default();\n\n    // Check database\n    let (db_issue_count, db_integrity_ok) = check_database(&db_path)?;\n    result.db_issue_count = db_issue_count;\n    if !db_integrity_ok {\n        db_ok = false;\n        issues.push(\"Database integrity check failed\".to_string());\n    }\n\n    // Check JSONL file\n    let (jsonl_line_count, jsonl_valid) = check_jsonl(&jsonl_path)?;\n    result.jsonl_line_count = jsonl_line_count;\n    if !jsonl_valid {\n        jsonl_ok = false;\n        issues.push(\"JSONL file contains invalid lines\".to_string());\n    }\n\n    // Check consistency with content_hash comparison\n    if db_ok && jsonl_ok {\n        let drift = check_consistency_with_hash(&db_path, &jsonl_path)?;\n\n        let total_drift = drift.missing_in_jsonl.len()\n            + drift.missing_in_sqlite.len()\n            + drift.hash_mismatch.len();\n\n        result.missing_in_jsonl = drift.missing_in_jsonl.clone();\n        result.missing_in_sqlite = drift.missing_in_sqlite.clone();\n        result.hash_mismatch = drift.hash_mismatch.clone();\n\n        if total_drift > 0 {\n            issues.push(format!(\n                \"Drift detected: {} missing in JSONL, {} missing in SQLite, {} hash mismatch\",\n                result.missing_in_jsonl.len(),\n                result.missing_in_sqlite.len(),\n                result.hash_mismatch.len()\n            ));\n        }\n\n        // Count unflushed beads\n        let unflushed_count = count_unflushed(&db_path)?;\n        result.unflushed_count = unflushed_count;\n\n        if unflushed_count > 0 {\n            issues.push(format!(\n                \"{} unflushed bead(s) exist (modified or created since last flush to JSONL)\",\n                unflushed_count\n            ));\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for beads stuck in a done-sounding custom status instead of closed/tombstone\n    // (bf-wre). Independent of db_ok/jsonl_ok since it only needs the sqlite db to be\n    // openable, not a full consistency check.\n    let pseudo_terminal_ids = check_pseudo_terminal_statuses(&db_path)?;\n    if !pseudo_terminal_ids.is_empty() {\n        issues.push(format!(\n            \"{} bead(s) have a done-sounding status (e.g. \\\"completed\\\") instead of \\\"closed\\\" \\\n             -- run `bf close <id>` on each to fully close them: {}\",\n            pseudo_terminal_ids.len(),\n            pseudo_terminal_ids.join(\", \")\n        ));\n    }\n    result.pseudo_terminal_status_ids = pseudo_terminal_ids;\n\n    result.db_ok = db_ok;\n    result.jsonl_ok = jsonl_ok;\n    result.issues = issues;\n\n    Ok(result)\n}\n\n/// Find beads whose status is a `TERMINAL_STATUS_ALIASES` value (e.g. \"completed\",\n/// \"done\") rather than the canonical \"closed\"/\"tombstone\". These beads are functionally\n/// terminal (see `Status::is_terminal()`) but weren't actually closed, so `bf show`/`bf\n/// list` still displays them as active and `closed_at`/`close_reason` are never set.\nfn check_pseudo_terminal_statuses(db_path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<String>> {\n    let conn = Connection::open(db_path)?;\n    let placeholders = crate::model::TERMINAL_STATUS_ALIASES\n        .iter()\n        .map(|s| format!(\"'{s}'\"))\n        .collect::<Vec<_>>()\n        .join(\", \");\n    let query = format!(\n        \"SELECT id FROM issues WHERE LOWER(status) IN ({placeholders}) AND deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY id\"\n    );\n    let mut stmt = conn.prepare(&query)?;\n    let ids = stmt\n        .query_map([], |row| row.get::<_, String>(0))?\n        .collect::<rusqlite::Result<Vec<_>>>()?;\n    Ok(ids)\n}\n\n/// Check database integrity.\nfn check_database(db_path: &Path) -> Result<(usize, bool)> {\n    let conn = Connection::open(db_path)?;\n\n    // Apply schema if database is new (no tables yet)\n    let needs_schema: bool = conn\n        .query_row(\n            \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='issues'\",\n            [],\n            |row| row.get::<_, i64>(0),\n        )\n        .map(|n| n == 0)\n        .unwrap_or(true);\n    if needs_schema {\n        crate::storage::schema::apply_schema(&conn)?;\n    }\n\n    // Run PRAGMA integrity_check\n    let integrity_result: String =\n        conn.query_row(\"PRAGMA integrity_check\", [], |row| row.get(0))?;\n\n    let integrity_ok = integrity_result == \"ok\";\n\n    // Count issues\n    let count: i64 = conn.query_row(\n        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM issues WHERE deleted_at IS NULL\",\n        [],\n        |row| row.get(0),\n    )?;\n\n    Ok((count as usize, integrity_ok))\n}\n\n/// Check JSONL file validity.\nfn check_jsonl(jsonl_path: &Path) -> Result<(usize, bool)> {\n    if !jsonl_path.exists() {\n        return Ok((0, true)); // Empty workspace is valid\n    }\n\n    let mut count = 0;\n    let mut valid = true;\n\n    match stream_issues(jsonl_path) {\n        Ok(iter) => {\n            for result in iter {\n                match result {\n                    Ok(_) => count += 1,\n                    Err(e) => {\n                        valid = false;\n                        eprintln!(\"Invalid JSONL line: {}\", e);\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n        Err(e) => {\n            return Err(anyhow!(\"Failed to read JSONL file: {}\", e));\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok((count, valid))\n}\n\n/// Consistency drift details.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]\nstruct ConsistencyDrift {\n    /// Bead IDs that exist in SQLite but not in JSONL\n    missing_in_jsonl: Vec<String>,\n    /// Bead IDs that exist in JSONL but not in SQLite\n    missing_in_sqlite: Vec<String>,\n    /// Bead IDs where content_hash differs between JSONL and SQLite\n    hash_mismatch: Vec<String>,\n}\n\n/// Check consistency between database and JSONL using content_hash.\n///\n/// Compares each bead's content_hash between JSONL and SQLite to detect drift.\nfn check_consistency_with_hash(db_path: &Path, jsonl_path: &Path) -> Result<ConsistencyDrift> {\n    use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};\n\n    let storage = Storage::open(db_path)?;\n\n    // Build a map of all SQLite issues with their content_hash\n    let sqlite_issues: HashMap<String, String> = storage\n        .list_all_issues()?\n        .into_iter()\n        .map(|issue| {\n            let hash = issue.content_hash();\n            (issue.id, hash)\n        })\n        .collect();\n\n    let mut drift = ConsistencyDrift::default();\n    let mut jsonl_seen: HashSet<String> = HashSet::new();\n\n    // Stream JSONL and compare, tracking seen IDs\n    if jsonl_path.exists() {\n        let iter = stream_issues(jsonl_path)?;\n        for result in iter {\n            let jsonl_issue = result?;\n            let jsonl_hash = jsonl_issue.content_hash();\n            let bead_id = jsonl_issue.id.clone();\n            jsonl_seen.insert(bead_id.clone());\n\n            match sqlite_issues.get(&bead_id) {\n                Some(sqlite_hash) => {\n                    if sqlite_hash != &jsonl_hash {\n                        drift.hash_mismatch.push(bead_id);\n                    }\n                }\n                None => {\n                    drift.missing_in_sqlite.push(bead_id);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Find beads in SQLite but not in JSONL (using tracked seen IDs)\n    for bead_id in sqlite_issues.keys() {\n        if !jsonl_seen.contains(bead_id) {\n            drift.missing_in_jsonl.push(bead_id.clone());\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(drift)\n}\n\n/// Count unflushed beads (db-only or db-newer beads not yet flushed to JSONL).\n///\n/// Returns the count of beads that exist in SQLite but have been marked as dirty\n/// (modified or created since the last flush to JSONL).\npub fn count_unflushed(db_path: &Path) -> Result<usize> {\n    use rusqlite::Connection;\n\n    let conn = Connection::open(db_path)?;\n\n    // Check if dirty_issues table exists\n    let table_exists: i64 = conn.query_row(\n        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='dirty_issues'\",\n        [],\n        |row| row.get(0),\n    )?;\n\n    if table_exists == 0 {\n        return Ok(0);\n    }\n\n    let count: i64 = conn.query_row(\n        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM dirty_issues\",\n        [],\n        |row| row.get(0),\n    )?;\n\n    Ok(count as usize)\n}\n\n/// Get IDs of unflushed beads.\n///\n/// Returns a list of bead IDs that exist in SQLite but have been marked as dirty\n/// (modified or created since the last flush to JSONL).\nfn get_unflushed_ids(db_path: &Path) -> Result<Vec<String>> {\n    use rusqlite::Connection;\n\n    let conn = Connection::open(db_path)?;\n\n    // Check if dirty_issues table exists\n    let table_exists: i64 = conn.query_row(\n        \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name='dirty_issues'\",\n        [],\n        |row| row.get(0),\n    )?;\n\n    if table_exists == 0 {\n        return Ok(Vec::new());\n    }\n\n    let mut stmt = conn.prepare(\"SELECT issue_id FROM dirty_issues ORDER BY issue_id\")?;\n    let mut rows = stmt.query([])?;\n\n    let mut ids = Vec::new();\n    while let Some(row) = rows.next()? {\n        ids.push(row.get(0)?);\n    }\n\n    Ok(ids)\n}\n\n/// Repair the database by rebuilding from JSONL.\n///\n/// This is the recovery operation when the database is corrupted or missing.\n/// The JSONL file is the authoritative source of truth for repair.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `workspace_dir` - Path to the workspace root (contains .beads/)\n/// * `flush_first` - If true, flush unflushed beads to JSONL before repairing\n/// * `force` - If true, proceed even with unflushed beads (they will be lost)\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(usize)` - Number of beads imported\npub fn repair(workspace_dir: &Path, flush_first: bool, force: bool) -> Result<usize> {\n    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir)\n        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display()))?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n    let jsonl_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.jsonl_export);\n\n    // Check if JSONL exists\n    if !jsonl_path.exists() {\n        return Err(anyhow!(\n            \"Cannot repair: JSONL file not found at {}\",\n            jsonl_path.display()\n        ));\n    }\n\n    // Check for unflushed beads if database exists and is valid\n    // If db is corrupted, we can't detect unflushed beads - proceed with warning\n    let (unflushed_ids, db_corrupted) = if db_path.exists() {\n        match get_unflushed_ids(&db_path) {\n            Ok(ids) => (ids, false),\n            Err(_) => {\n                // Database is corrupted or unreadable\n                // We can't detect unflushed beads, so proceed with a warning\n                if flush_first {\n                    // Cannot flush from a corrupt database\n                    return Err(anyhow!(\n                        \"Cannot flush: database is corrupted and unreadable.\\n\\\n                         Flushing from a corrupt DB would poison the JSONL checkpoint.\\n\\\n                         Unflushed beads cannot be recovered.\\n\\\n                         Remove --flush-first to proceed with repair only.\"\n                    ));\n                }\n                // Proceed without unflushed check (db is unreadable)\n                // No --force needed since we can't detect unflushed beads anyway\n                eprintln!(\"WARNING: Database is corrupted and unreadable.\");\n                eprintln!(\"  Cannot detect unflushed beads - any db-only beads will be lost.\");\n                eprintln!(\"  Proceeding with repair from JSONL...\");\n                (Vec::new(), true)\n            }\n        }\n    } else {\n        // DB doesn't exist - no unflushed beads possible\n        (Vec::new(), false)\n    };\n\n    if !unflushed_ids.is_empty() {\n        if flush_first {\n            // Flush ALL beads to JSONL first (not just dirty ones)\n            // We must export everything because repair will rebuild db from JSONL\n            eprintln!(\"Flushing all beads to JSONL before repair (including {} unflushed)...\", unflushed_ids.len());\n            let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n            let flushed = storage.sync_to_jsonl(&jsonl_path, false)?;\n            eprintln!(\"Flushed {} bead(s) to JSONL\", flushed);\n        } else if !force {\n            // Refuse to proceed with unflushed beads\n            return Err(anyhow!(\n                \"Cannot repair: {} unflushed bead(s) exist ({}).\\n\\\n                 Run 'bf doctor --repair --flush-first' to flush before repair,\\n\\\n                 or 'bf doctor --repair --force' to proceed (these beads will be LOST).\\n\\\n                 Unflushed beads: {}\",\n                unflushed_ids.len(),\n                if unflushed_ids.len() <= 5 {\n                    unflushed_ids.iter().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(\", \")\n                } else {\n                    format!(\n                        \"{}, ... and {} more\",\n                        unflushed_ids[..5].join(\", \"),\n                        unflushed_ids.len() - 5\n                    )\n                },\n                unflushed_ids.join(\", \")\n            ));\n        } else {\n            // Force mode: warn but proceed\n            eprintln!(\n                \"WARNING: {} unflushed bead(s) will be LOST: {}\",\n                unflushed_ids.len(),\n                if unflushed_ids.len() <= 5 {\n                    unflushed_ids.iter().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(\", \")\n                } else {\n                    format!(\n                        \"{}, ... and {} more\",\n                        unflushed_ids[..5].join(\", \"),\n                        unflushed_ids.len() - 5\n                    )\n                }\n            );\n            eprintln!(\"Proceeding with repair due to --force flag\");\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Backup existing database if it exists\n    if db_path.exists() {\n        let backup_path = db_path.with_extension(&format!(\n            \"db.backup.{}\",\n            chrono::Utc::now().format(\"%Y%m%d%H%M%S\")\n        ));\n        std::fs::copy(&db_path, &backup_path)?;\n        eprintln!(\"Backed up existing database to {}\", backup_path.display());\n    }\n\n    // Delete old database\n    if db_path.exists() {\n        std::fs::remove_file(&db_path)?;\n    }\n\n    // Create new database and import JSONL\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let result = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n        import_jsonl(&jsonl_path, |issue| {\n            Storage::create_issue_tx(tx, &issue)?;\n            Ok(UpsertResult::New)\n        })\n    })?;\n\n    // Rebuild blocked cache\n    storage.rebuild_blocked_cache()?;\n\n    // Clear dirty marks - after rebuild from JSONL, db and JSONL are in sync\n    // (create_issue_tx doesn't mark dirty, but we clear for safety)\n    storage.clear_dirty()?;\n\n    Ok(result.imported)\n}\n\n/// Rebuild the blocked issues cache.\n///\n/// This materialized view should be rebuilt after dependency or status changes.\npub fn rebuild_cache(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<()> {\n    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir)\n        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display()))?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n    storage.rebuild_blocked_cache()?;\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// Reclaim stale in_progress beads.\n///\n/// Resets beads that have been in_progress for longer than the TTL\n/// back to open status.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `workspace_dir` - Path to the workspace root (contains .beads/)\n/// * `ttl_minutes` - TTL in minutes (default from config is 30)\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(usize)` - Number of beads reclaimed\npub fn reclaim_stale(workspace_dir: &Path, ttl_minutes: i64) -> Result<usize> {\n    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir)\n        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display()))?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let reclaimed = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n        let stale_cutoff = chrono::Utc::now() - chrono::Duration::minutes(ttl_minutes);\n\n        let reclaimed = tx.execute(\n            \"UPDATE issues\n             SET status = 'open', assignee = NULL, updated_at = ?\n             WHERE status = 'in_progress' AND updated_at < ?\",\n            rusqlite::params![chrono::Utc::now().to_rfc3339(), stale_cutoff.to_rfc3339()],\n        )?;\n\n        Ok::<_, anyhow::Error>(reclaimed)\n    })?;\n\n    Ok(reclaimed)\n}\n\n/// Initialize a new database from an existing JSONL file.\n///\n/// Use this to create a fresh database from a JSONL export without\n/// affecting the existing database.\n///\n/// # Arguments\n/// * `workspace_dir` - Path to the workspace root (contains .beads/)\n/// * `jsonl_path` - Path to the JSONL file to import from\n///\n/// # Returns\n/// * `Ok(usize)` - Number of beads imported\npub fn init_from_jsonl(workspace_dir: &Path, jsonl_path: &Path) -> Result<usize> {\n    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir)\n        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display()))?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n\n    // Check if JSONL exists\n    if !jsonl_path.exists() {\n        return Err(anyhow!(\"JSONL file not found at {}\", jsonl_path.display()));\n    }\n\n    // Create new database and import JSONL\n    let storage = Storage::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    let result = storage.with_immediate_transaction(|tx| {\n        import_jsonl(jsonl_path, |issue| {\n            Storage::create_issue_tx(tx, &issue)?;\n            Ok(UpsertResult::New)\n        })\n    })?;\n\n    // Rebuild blocked cache\n    storage.rebuild_blocked_cache()?;\n\n    // Clear dirty marks - after import from JSONL, db and JSONL are in sync\n    // (create_issue_tx doesn't mark dirty, but we clear for safety)\n    storage.clear_dirty()?;\n\n    Ok(result.imported)\n}\n\n/// Verify database schema version.\n///\n/// Checks that all required tables and indexes exist.\npub fn verify_schema(workspace_dir: &Path) -> Result<bool> {\n    let beads_dir = find_beads_dir(workspace_dir)\n        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!(\"No .beads directory found in {}\", workspace_dir.display()))?;\n    let metadata = load_metadata(&beads_dir)?;\n    let db_path = beads_dir.join(&metadata.database);\n\n    let conn = Connection::open(&db_path)?;\n\n    // Check for critical tables\n    let tables = [\n        \"issues\",\n        \"dependencies\",\n        \"labels\",\n        \"comments\",\n        \"events\",\n        \"config\",\n        \"metadata\",\n        \"dirty_issues\",\n        \"export_hashes\",\n        \"blocked_issues_cache\",\n        \"child_counters\",\n    ];\n\n    for table in &tables {\n        let exists: i64 = conn.query_row(\n            \"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table' AND name=?1\",\n            &[table],\n            |row| row.get(0),\n        )?;\n\n        if exists == 0 {\n            eprintln!(\"Missing table: {}\", table);\n            return Ok(false);\n        }\n    }\n\n    Ok(true)\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use crate::config::init_workspace;\n    use crate::jsonl::export_jsonl;\n    use crate::model::{Issue, IssueType, Priority, Status};\n    use tempfile::TempDir;\n\n    #[test]\n    fn test_check_empty_workspace() {\n        let temp_dir =
